Svaka lekcija rešava jedan deo sistema.

Ali Lekcija 12 povezuje sve.

Bez ovog finalnog sloja, kompanija ima aktivnost.

Sa ovim slojem, kompanija ima pipeline motor.


## Inbound-to-Outbound okvir


### Attract → Capture → Score → Route → Personalize → Follow Up → Report

Ovo je kompletan sistem.


#### Korak 1: Attract

Šta uraditi: Koristi founder content, profile, komentare, webinare, lead magnete i ads da kreiraš pažnju.
Zašto je važno: Bez pažnje nema signala.
Primer: Founder post, webinar invite, Thought Leader Ad, lead magnet.
Česta greška: Kreiranje content-a bez capture mehanizma.
Probaj ovo: Svaki content asset treba da ima jasan next step.
Istaknuto: Attract kreira pažnju.


#### Korak 2: Capture

Šta uraditi: Prikupi signale sa LinkedIn-a, formi, webinara, website poseta i CRM aktivnosti.
Zašto je važno: Signali bez capture-a se gube.
Primer: Komentar, registracija, download, pricing visit, DM.
Česta greška: Signali ostaju u alatima, ne u CRM-u.
Probaj ovo: Centralizuj signale u CRM ili staging listu.
Istaknuto: Capture hvata signal.


#### Korak 3: Score

Šta uraditi: Evaluiraj ICP fit, signal strength, topic, recency i buyer role.
Zašto je važno: Score određuje prioritet.
Primer: High fit + strong signal + relevant topic = priority action.
Česta greška: Delovanje na svaki signal jednako.
Probaj ovo: Koristi fit + signal pre nego što pošalješ outreach.
Istaknuto: Score kvantifikuje intent.


#### Korak 4: Route

Šta uraditi: Odluči ko dobija nurture, ko DM, ko email follow-up, i ko postaje sales task.
Zašto je važno: Routing sprečava random follow-up.
Primer: High fit + medium signal = contextual DM. High fit + strong = sales task.
Česta greška: Routing samo po signalu, bez ICP fit-a.
Probaj ovo: Kreiraj routing tabelu i drži se nje.
Istaknuto: Route određuje akciju.


#### Korak 5: Personalize

Šta uraditi: Napiši outreach na osnovu signala i teme.
Zašto je važno: Kontekst povećava reply rate.
Primer: Webinar question → reference question + send checklist.
Česta greška: Ista „book a call“ poruka za sve.
Probaj ovo: Počni sa usefulness, ne sa pitch-om.
Istaknuto: Personalize daje relevance.


#### Korak 6: Follow Up

Šta uraditi: Koristi kratke, poštovane sekvence koje nastavljaju razgovor.
Zašto je važno: Jedan touch retko zatvara deal.
Primer: Replay → DM → email → soft invite.
Česta greška: Over-automation warm lead-ova.
Probaj ovo: Automatizuj routing, ne kontekst poruka.
Istaknuto: Follow up nastavlja momentum.


#### Korak 7: Report

Šta uraditi: Prati conversations, opportunities, revenue influence i šta poboljšati.
Zašto je važno: Bez report-a ne znaš šta radi.
Primer: Signal-to-conversation, content-to-pipeline, webinar-to-revenue.
Česta greška: Reporting samo impressions i registracija.
Probaj ovo: Prati signal → conversation → qualified → opportunity → revenue.
Istaknuto: Report zatvara learning loop.

[NAPOMENA] Ovo je trenutak kada Founder-Led Growth prestaje da bude content i postaje revenue infrastruktura.


#### How To Create a B2B Content Strategy that ACTUALLY Converts

Kanal: YouTube
Relevantno za povezivanje content-a sa konverzijom i sales ishodima.
Napomena: Pogledaj posle framework sekcije ili u video sekciji.
Link: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QLsrenrNDe0


## Korak po korak: sistem setup


#### Korak 1: Definiši signale koji trigger-uju akciju

Šta uraditi: Izaberi signale koji treba da pomeraju nekoga od inbound pažnje do outbound follow-up-a.

Signal map:
| Signal | Strength | Next action |
| Like from ICP | Light | Monitor / engage back |
| Comment from ICP | Medium | Reply + add to warm list |
| Repeat engagement | Medium | Soft DM or invite to webinar |
| Profile view after outreach | Medium | Follow-up with context |
| Lead magnet download | Medium | Send related resource |
| Webinar registration | Medium | Pre-event nurture |
| Webinar attendance | Medium/strong | Send replay + checklist |
| Webinar question | Strong | Founder DM |
| CTA click | Strong | Sales task |
| Pricing/demo page visit | Strong | Sales follow-up |
| DM asking for help | Strong | Qualify and book call |
Zašto je važno: Bez signal map-e, follow-up je haotičan.
Primer: ICP comment → reply + warm list. Webinar question → founder DM.
Česta greška: Tretiranje svake inbound akcije kao sales-ready.
Probaj ovo: Kreiraj tri action level-a: nurture, soft outreach, sales follow-up.
Istaknuto: Signali određuju akciju.


#### Korak 2: Kreiraj routing logiku

Šta uraditi: Odluči šta se dešava posle svakog signala.

Routing table:
| Fit | Signal strength | Route |
| Low fit | Any signal | Ignore or low-priority nurture |
| Medium fit | Light signal | Monitor |
| Medium fit | Medium signal | Nurture / invite to webinar |
| Medium fit | Strong signal | Review manually |
| High fit | Light signal | Engage back |
| High fit | Medium signal | Contextual DM or resource |
| High fit | Strong signal | Sales task or founder follow-up |
Zašto je važno: Routing sprečava pogrešan handoff.
Primer: High fit + medium signal = contextual DM.
Česta greška: Routing samo po signalu, bez ICP fit-a.
Probaj ovo: Nikad ne rutiraj lead u sales samo zbog ponašanja. Zahtevaj fit + signal.
Istaknuto: Fit + signal = route.


#### Korak 3: Izgradi CRM polja

Šta uraditi: Dodaj polja koja omogućavaju signal-based follow-up.

Minimum fields:
| Field | Example |
| Lead source | LinkedIn, webinar, ad, website, referral |
| First touchpoint | Founder post, comment, webinar, lead magnet |
| Signal type | Comment, download, attendance, CTA click |
| Signal strength | Light, medium, strong |
| Content topic | ICP, profile, content, outreach, webinar, CRM, signals |
| ICP fit | High, medium, low |
| Buyer role | Founder, VP Sales, RevOps, Head of Growth |
| Next best action | Engage, DM, email, call, nurture |
| Follow-up date | Date |
| Campaign / asset | Webinar, checklist, playbook, ad |
| Pipeline influence | LinkedIn, webinar, ad, outreach |
Zašto je važno: CRM polja omogućavaju routing i reporting.
Primer: Signal type + topic + fit + next action.
Česta greška: Gradnja automatizacija pre nego što CRM polja postoje.
Probaj ovo: Prvo kreiraj polja. Zatim gradí workflow-e.
Istaknuto: CRM = system memory.


#### Korak 4: Kreiraj kontekstualne outreach play-e

Šta uraditi: Napiši follow-up poruke na osnovu signala.

LinkedIn comment: „Hey [Name], saw your comment on the post about [topic]. The point you raised around [specific detail] is exactly where a lot of [ICP] get stuck. I have a short [resource] on this if useful.“

Webinar: „Hey [Name], thanks for joining the session on [topic]. You mentioned [poll answer / question / topic]. I can send the checklist we used in the session if helpful.“

Lead magnet: „Hey [Name], saw you grabbed the [resource]. Most teams use it when they're trying to solve [problem]. If useful, I can send a quick example of how we apply it with [ICP].“

Website: „Hey [Name], teams usually reach this stage when they're comparing how to solve [problem]. Happy to send a breakdown of the main options and trade-offs if useful.“
Zašto je važno: Kontekstualne poruke povećavaju reply rate.
Primer: Webinar question → reference question + send framework.
Česta greška: Ista „book a call“ poruka za svaki inbound lead.
Probaj ovo: Počni sa usefulness. Pređi na call tek kad postoji intent.
Istaknuto: Context beats cold pitch.


#### Korak 5: Izgradi kratke follow-up sekvence

Šta uraditi: Kreiraj jednostavne, poštovane sekvence po tipu signala.

Webinar attendee sequence:
| Touch | Channel | Message |
| 1 | Email | Replay + checklist |
| 2 | LinkedIn DM | Reference poll/question |
| 3 | Email | Practical example or template |
| 4 | LinkedIn | Soft consultation invite |
| 5 | Email | Final useful resource / nurture |

Content engager sequence:
| Touch | Channel | Message |
| 1 | LinkedIn | Reference post engagement + resource |
| 2 | LinkedIn comment | Continue public relationship |
| 3 | Email | Send related checklist |
| 4 | LinkedIn DM | Ask relevant diagnostic question |
| 5 | Nurture | Add to topic-based content flow |
Zašto je važno: Sekvence održavaju momentum bez spam-a.
Primer: 5-touch webinar sequence sa replay, DM, email, invite.
Česta greška: Over-automation warm lead-ova.
Probaj ovo: Koristi automation za routing i reminders. Koristi human context za poruke.
Istaknuto: Short sequences win.


#### Korak 6: Kreiraj nedeljni operating rhythm

Šta uraditi: Vodi sistem svake nedelje.

Weekly rhythm:
| Day | Action |
| Monday | Review last week's signals and pipeline movement |
| Tuesday | Publish problem-led content |
| Wednesday | Follow up with warm signals |
| Thursday | Run engagement and outreach review |
| Friday | CRM cleanup, report, and next week planning |

Weekly review questions:
- Which content created the best signals?
- Which webinar topic created intent?
- Which signals turned into replies?
- Which outreach messages worked?
- Which leads need nurture?
- Which accounts should sales prioritize?
- Which topic should we repeat next week?
Zašto je važno: Sistem bez ritma postaje neaktivan.
Primer: 30-min Friday pipeline review.
Česta greška: Gradnja sistema ali ne operisanje.
Probaj ovo: Zakaži 30-min nedeljni Founder-Led Growth pipeline review.
Istaknuto: Weekly rhythm = operating system.


#### Korak 7: Report-uj pipeline movement

Šta uraditi: Prati da li inbound-to-outbound kreira revenue.

Reports:
| Report | What it shows |
| Signal-to-conversation report | Which signals create replies |
| Content-to-pipeline report | Which topics create opportunities |
| Webinar-to-revenue report | Which webinars influence deals |
| Ad-to-lead-quality report | Which paid boosts create qualified leads |
| Outreach reply report | Which context creates replies |
| Nurture reactivation report | Which "not now" leads return |
| Revenue influence report | Which activities influenced closed deals |
Zašto je važno: Reporting pokazuje šta poboljšati.
Primer: Signal → conversation → qualified → opportunity → revenue.
Česta greška: Reporting samo impressions, registracija i lead-ova.
Probaj ovo: Prati pun path: signal → conversation → qualified → opportunity → revenue.
Istaknuto: Report movement, not activity.

Signal map

Signal | Strength | Next action
--- | --- | ---
Like from ICP | Light | Monitor / engage back
Comment from ICP | Medium | Reply + add to warm list
Repeat engagement | Medium | Soft DM or invite to webinar
Profile view after outreach | Medium | Follow-up with context
Lead magnet download | Medium | Send related resource
Webinar registration | Medium | Pre-event nurture
Webinar attendance | Medium/strong | Send replay + checklist
Webinar question | Strong | Founder DM
CTA click | Strong | Sales task
Pricing/demo page visit | Strong | Sales follow-up
DM asking for help | Strong | Qualify and book call

Routing logika

Fit | Signal strength | Route
--- | --- | ---
Low fit | Any signal | Ignore or low-priority nurture
Medium fit | Light signal | Monitor
Medium fit | Medium signal | Nurture / invite to webinar
Medium fit | Strong signal | Review manually
High fit | Light signal | Engage back
High fit | Medium signal | Contextual DM or resource
High fit | Strong signal | Sales task or founder follow-up


#### What Is Inbound Marketing?

Kanal: YouTube
Korisno za razumevanje inbound strane sistema: privlačenje, konverzija i nurture kupaca.
Napomena: Pogledaj posle core sekcije ili u video sekciji.
Link: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=R0UWvdyABFo


## Mesto u Lead Agents sistemu

Lekcija 12 zatvara ceo Lead Agents playbook: jedna petlja od ICP-a do pipeline-a.

Redosled: ICP dokument → profil → cold outreach → buying signali → content (intervjui) → Thought Leader Ads → CRM sa lead scoring-om.

Operativno: outreach povezuje, content gradi poverenje, signali pokreću re-engagement, value-based follow-up sistem drži one bez odgovora dok nisu spremni.

CRM routing: no_reply → signal_reengage → nurture → meeting. Outreach direktan, ads indirektan. Cilj su prave LinkedIn konekcije, ne lista kontakata bez konteksta.


## Dokaz i istraživanja


#### Inbound usklađuje content, kanale i automation sa buyer journey-em

HubSpot objašnjava inbound marketing kao privlačenje kvalifikovanih prospect-a relevantnim content-om, konverziju u lead-ove i kupce, i nurture kroz buyer journey.
Izvor: HubSpot — https://www.hubspot.com/inbound-marketing


#### B2B kupovina je nelinearna i zahteva digital + human interakciju

Gartner opisuje B2B buying kao nelinearan, gde kupci ponovo posete buying jobs poput identifikacije problema, istraživanja rešenja, definisanja zahteva i izbora dobavljača kroz digitalne i ljudske interakcije.
Izvor: Gartner — https://www.gartner.com/en/sales/insights/b2b-buying-journey


#### Većina kupaca su future buyers, memory je važna

LinkedIn B2B Institute 95-5 Rule objašnjava da većina potencijalnih B2B kupaca nije in-market u datom trenutku, pa brendovi treba da kreiraju memory pre nego što se buying window otvori.
Izvor: LinkedIn B2B Institute — https://business.linkedin.com/advertise/resources/b2b-institute/b2b-research/trends/95-5-rule


#### Attribution povezuje interakcije sa revenue

HubSpot attribution reporting analizira interakcije poput klikova, formi, page views i source-a, dok Salesforce Campaign Influence povezuje kampanje sa opportunities i revenue influence.
Izvor: HubSpot — https://knowledge.hubspot.com/reports/understand-attribution-reporting


#### HubSpot CRM Tutorial for Beginners

Kanal: YouTube
Korisno za izgradnju CRM sloja inbound-to-outbound sistema.
Napomena: Pogledaj posle system-steps sekcije ili u video sekciji.
Link: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GBS7XK3H4k4


## Kako implementirati

1. Mapiraj trenutni sistem

Channel audit

Channel | Current activity | Signal captured? | CRM field? | Follow-up owner? | Reported?
--- | --- | --- | --- | --- | ---
LinkedIn content | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]
LinkedIn comments | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]
Webinars | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]
Lead magnets | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]
LinkedIn Ads | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]
Website visits | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]
Outreach | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]
CRM | [yes/no] | [yes/no] | [field] | [owner] | [yes/no]

Praznine pokazuju gde sistem puca.

2. Definiši signal-to-action pravila

Signal-to-action rules

Signal | Fit required | Action | Owner | SLA
--- | --- | --- | --- | ---
ICP comment | Medium/high | Reply + add to warm list | Founder / Sales | 24h
Repeat engagement | High | Soft DM | Founder / Sales | 48h
Webinar registration | Medium/high | Pre-event nurture | Marketing | Same day
Webinar question | High | Founder DM | Founder | 24h
Lead magnet download | Medium/high | Send related resource | Sales / Marketing | 24h
Pricing/demo visit | High | Sales task | Sales | Same day
CTA click | High | Sales follow-up | Sales | Same day
„Not now“ reply | Medium/high | Nurture task | Sales | 30-90 days

3. Izgradi topic-based follow-up paths

Topic paths

Topic | Asset | Follow-up
--- | --- | ---
ICP | ICP template | Ask if they want help narrowing segment
Profile | Profile checklist | Offer quick profile audit
Content | Content pillar template | Ask what they are posting about now
Commenting | Comment strategy template | Ask if they have target engagement list
Outreach | Warm outreach framework | Ask how they currently build lists
Webinar | Webinar structure template | Ask if they are running sessions now
CRM | CRM field map | Ask how they track LinkedIn signals
Buying signals | Signal scorecard | Ask which signals they can currently see

4. Izgradi CRM automation layer

Automations

Trigger | Automation
--- | ---
Form submit | Create/update contact + tag topic
Webinar registration | Add webinar campaign + status
Webinar attendance | Update signal strength
CTA click | Create sales task
Lead magnet download | Send resource + create follow-up
High score | Alert sales in Slack
No activity after outreach | Nurture task
„Not now“ reply | Follow-up date in 60 days

Koristi automation da smanjiš admin.

Ne koristi automation da ukloniš kontekst.

5. Kreiraj outbound playbook

Outbound treba da referencira inbound ponašanje.

LinkedIn engagement:

„Hey [Name], noticed you engaged with the post about [topic]. We see this a lot with [ICP]: [problem]. I have a short [resource] if useful.“

Webinar:

„Hey [Name], thanks for joining the session on [topic]. You mentioned [poll/question/topic]. I can send the framework we used if helpful.“

Lead magnet:

„Hey [Name], saw you downloaded the [asset]. Most teams use it when they're trying to solve [problem]. Want me to send an example of how we apply it?“

High intent:

„Hey [Name], based on what you engaged with around [topic], it looks like [problem] might be relevant. Worth comparing notes for 15 minutes?“

6. Kreiraj finalni dashboard

Dashboard

Section | Metrics
--- | ---
Attention | profile views, post engagement, followers, ad engagement
Capture | form fills, webinar registrations, lead magnet downloads
Intent | questions, CTA clicks, pricing visits, repeat engagement
Action | DMs sent, emails sent, tasks created
Conversation | replies, resource requests, calls booked
Pipeline | qualified leads, opportunities, proposals
Revenue | won deals, revenue influenced, source/topic influence

[NAPOMENA] Dashboard treba da pokazuje kretanje, ne samo aktivnost.

## Idealni stack alata


#### LinkedIn

Za šta: Founder content, komentari, profile visits, DM-ovi i relationship-building.
Zašto odgovara: Glavni izvor Founder-Led Growth pažnje i signala.
Alternativa: None


#### LinkedIn Sales Navigator

Za šta: ICP targeting, saved leads, account monitoring i sales research.
Zašto odgovara: Pomaže da LinkedIn aktivnost postane account-based sales workflow.
Alternativa: Apollo, manual LinkedIn search


#### HubSpot

Za šta: CRM, forme, landing pages, workflow-i, attribution, pipeline reporting i email nurture.
Zašto odgovara: Jak centralni hub za inbound-to-outbound workflow-e.
Alternativa: Pipedrive, Salesforce, Attio


#### Pipedrive

Za šta: Sales pipeline, custom fields, task-ovi i reporting.
Zašto odgovara: Jednostavan CRM za founder-led timove koji trebaju sales clarity.
Alternativa: HubSpot, Attio


#### Attio

Za šta: Fleksibilni CRM objekti, relationships, liste i GTM workflow-i.
Zašto odgovara: Dobar za custom founder-led sisteme i relationship tracking.
Alternativa: HubSpot, Pipedrive


#### Clay

Za šta: Enrichment, trigger detection, list building i signal-based routing.
Zašto odgovara: Povezuje inbound signale sa outbound-ready data.
Alternativa: Apollo, Common Room


#### Apollo

Za šta: Contact data, prospecting, email enrichment i outbound liste.
Zašto odgovara: Korisno za pretvaranje kvalifikovanih inbound signala u outbound-ready kontakte.
Alternativa: Clay, Cognism, ZoomInfo


#### HeyReach

Za šta: LinkedIn outreach i multi-account LinkedIn sekvence.
Zašto odgovara: Korisno za slanje kontekstualnih LinkedIn DM-ova kad su liste kvalifikovane.
Alternativa: LaGrowthMachine, manual LinkedIn outreach


#### Lemlist

Za šta: Email i multichannel outreach.
Zašto odgovara: Korisno za kratke follow-up sekvence posle kvalifikovanih inbound signala.
Alternativa: LaGrowthMachine, Instantly


#### Zoom Webinars / Livestorm

Za šta: Webinar registracija, attendance, engagement i follow-up.
Zašto odgovara: Webinari kreiraju high-intent signale.
Alternativa: Demio, Riverside


#### Make.com / Zapier

Za šta: Povezivanje formi, webinara, CRM-a, outreach alata, Slack-a i reporting-a.
Zašto odgovara: Automatizuje signal-to-action workflow.
Alternativa: n8n, native integrations


#### Google Sheets

Za šta: Manual staging liste, rani eksperimenti i signal scoring.
Zašto odgovara: Dobar pre nego što potpuno automatizuješ.
Alternativa: Notion, CRM list views


## Primer workflow-a


### Od content-a do pipeline-a u jednom sistemu

1. Founder objavi post o specifičnom ICP pain-u.
2. ICP buyer komentariše.
3. Founder odgovori i kontakt se dodaje kao Engaged.
4. Buyer se kasnije registruje na webinar o istoj temi.
5. Webinar poll otkriva njihov bottleneck.
6. CRM ažurira signal strength i content topic.
7. Buyer postavi pitanje tokom webinara.
8. Founder pošalje kontekstualni DM sa template-om.
9. Sales pošalje kratak email follow-up sa istim kontekstom.
10. Buyer odgovori i pristane na call.
11. Deal se kreira i taguje sa LinkedIn + webinar influence.
12. Revenue report pokazuje koja content tema je pokrenula journey.

[ISTAKNUTO] To je Founder-Led Growth sistem. Content kreira signal. CRM ga rutira. Outbound nastavlja. Sales konvertuje.

## Pre nego što završiš playbook, uradi ovo


### Čeklista Lekcije 12

☐ Mapiraj svaki trenutni inbound source: LinkedIn, webinari, forme, website, ads, referral-i.
☐ Definiši koji signali treba da trigger-uju akciju.
☐ Razdvoji light, medium i strong signale.
☐ Dodaj CRM polja za source, signal, topic, fit, next action i follow-up date.
☐ Kreiraj routing rules na osnovu fit-a i signal strength-a.
☐ Izgradi topic-based follow-up paths.
☐ Napiši kontekstualne DM i email template-e.
☐ Kreiraj kratke sekvence za webinar attendees, content engagers i lead magnet downloads.
☐ Izgradi nedeljni operating rhythm.
☐ Kreiraj report-e za signal-to-conversation, content-to-pipeline, webinar-to-revenue i revenue influence.
☐ Dodeli ownere za svaku next action.
☐ Vodi sistem 30 dana pre nego što dodaš kompleksnost.

## ČESTA PITANJA

P: Šta je inbound-to-outbound sistem?
O: Inbound-to-outbound sistem koristi inbound ponašanje, poput content engagement-a, webinar attendance-a, lead magnet download-a, website poseta i CRM signala, da trigger-uje kontekstualni outbound follow-up. Umesto cold poruka svima, sales follow-up-uje kada postoji fit, signal i kontekst.

P: Kako se ovo razlikuje od normalnog outbound-a?
O: Normal outbound često počinje sa listom i porukom. Inbound-to-outbound počinje sa signalom. Kupac je već interagovao sa tvojim content-om, webinarom, profilom, ad-om ili website-om, pa poruka može referencirati stvarni kontekst.

P: Koji signali treba da trigger-uju outbound?
O: Jaki trigger-i uključuju: webinar pitanja; CTA klikove; pricing ili demo page posete; repeat engagement od target account-a; DM-ove; lead magnet download-e od high-fit buyer-a; komentare na founder postovima; relevantne company trigger-e.

P: Da li svaki inbound lead treba da dobije sales outreach?
O: Ne. Svaki inbound lead treba prvo da bude kvalifikovan. Koristi ICP fit, signal strength, topic relevance, recency i buyer role da odlučiš da li treba nurture, soft DM, sales follow-up ili no action.

P: Koji alati su potrebni?
O: Jednostavna verzija može raditi sa: LinkedIn; Google Sheets; HubSpot ili Pipedrive; Calendly; email; manual follow-up. Naprednija verzija može koristiti: Clay; Apollo; HeyReach; Lemlist; Make.com; Zapier; webinar alate; intent alate.

P: Koja je najveća greška u inbound-to-outbound-u?
O: Najveća greška je ne posedovanje handoff-a. Marketing kreira signale. Sales ne deluje. Founder razgovori ostaju u DM-ovima. CRM se ažurira prekasno. Topli lead-ovi hladnu. Sistem treba routing rules i ownere.

P: Kako znaš da sistem radi?
O: Prati: signal-to-conversation rate; content-to-pipeline; webinar-to-revenue; outreach replies po tipu signala; kvalifikovane opportunities; nurture reactivations; revenue influenced by Founder-Led Growth.

## ZAKLJUČAK / CTA

Founder-Led Growth postaje predvidljiv kada svaki signal ima next step
Founder profil bez content-a je pasivan. Content bez engagement-a je izolovan. Engagement bez CRM-a se zaboravlja. CRM bez outreach-a je samo baza. Outreach bez konteksta je cold. Webinari bez follow-up-a su protraćeni. Signali bez akcije su šum. Sistem radi kada se sve poveže. To je poenta Founder-Led Growth: pretvori founder ekspertizu u market trust, trust u signale, signale u razgovore, i razgovore u pipeline. Ako želiš pomoć da izgradiš ovaj sistem za svoju kompaniju, zakaži konsultaciju sa Lead Agents.
